using presentation::controls::*;
using presentation::controls::list::*;
using presentation::controls::tree::*;
using presentation::elements::*;
using presentation::elements::Gui*;
using presentation::elements::text::*;
using presentation::compositions::*;
using presentation::templates::*;
using presentation::theme::*;

namespace tuiskin
{
    @cpp:Friend(typeof(TuiThemeConstructor))
    class TuiTheme : ::presentation::theme::ThemeTemplates, TuiThemeConstructor
    {
        static func CreateBaselineDocument() : (DocumentModel^)
        {
            return tuiskin::CreateBaselineDocument();
        }

        static func GetColorPackage() : (ColorPackage)
        {
            return tuiColors;
        }

        new* ()
        {
            var <resource> = ::presentation::IGuiResourceManager::GetResourceManager().GetResourceFromClassName("tuiskin::TuiTheme");
            var <resolver> = new (::presentation::GuiResourcePathResolver^)(<resource>, <resource>.WorkingDirectory);
            this.SetResourceResolver(<resolver>);
            this.<tuiskin-TuiTheme>Initialize(this);
        }

        delete
        {
            FinalizeGeneralInstance(this);
        }
    }
}

            if ((<prop>Commands != <value>))
            {
                (<prop>Commands = <value>);
                CommandsChanged();

        }

        event CommandsChanged();

        prop Commands : ITabCommandExecutor*{GetCommands, SetCommands : CommandsChanged}

        @cpp:Private
        var <parameter>CurrentTabPage : presentation::controls::GuiTabPage* = null of (::presentation::controls::GuiTabPage*);

        func GetCurrentTabPage() : (presentation::controls::GuiTabPage*)
        {
            return <parameter>CurrentTabPage;
        }

        prop CurrentTabPage : presentation::controls::GuiTabPage*{GetCurrentTabPage}

        new* (<ctor-parameter>CurrentTabPage : presentation::controls::GuiTabPage*)
        {
            (<parameter>CurrentTabPage = <ctor-parameter>CurrentTabPage);
            var <resource> = ::presentation::IGuiResourceManager::GetResourceManager().GetResourceFromClassName("tuiskin::TuiTabHeaderTemplate");

            (self = <this>);
            {
                (self.MinSizeLimitation = LimitToElementAndChildren of (::presentation::compositions::GuiGraphicsComposition::MinSizeLimitation));
            }
            (<precompile>0 = new (::presentation::elements::GuiSolidBackgroundElement^)());
            {
                (self.OwnedElement = <precompile>0);
            }
            (container = new (::presentation::compositions::GuiBoundsComposition*)());
            <this>.SetNamedObject("container", container);
            {
                (container.AlignmentToParent = {left : 0; top : 0; right : 0; bottom : 0;} of (::presentation::Margin));
            }
            {
                (container.MinSizeLimitation = LimitToElementAndChildren of (::presentation::compositions::GuiGraphicsComposition::MinSizeLimitation));
            }
            {
                self.AddChild(container);
            }
            {
                (<precompile>0.Color = tuiskin::tuiColors.ControlBackground);
            }
            {
                (self.ContainerComposition = container);
            }
        }

        new ()
        {
        }
    }
}

module TuiPalette;
using presentation::*;

namespace tuiskin
{
    struct ColorPackage
    {
        ControlBackground : Color;
        ControlText : Color;
        ControlBorder : Color;
        ControlBorderDisabled : Color;
        ControlBorderFocused : Color;
        LabelText : Color;
        ShortcutKeyBackground : Color;
        ShortcutKeyText : Color;
        MenuBackground : Color;
        MenuText : Color;
        MenuTextDisabled : Color;
        MenuBackgroundHighlighted : Color;
        MenuTextHighlighted : Color;
        ItemBackground : Color;
        ItemText : Color;
        ItemTextDisabled : Color;
        ItemBackgroundHighlighted : Color;
        ItemTextHighlighted : Color;
        ItemBackgroundSelected : Color;
        ItemTextSelected : Color;
        ButtonBackground : Color;
        ButtonText : Color;
        ButtonIcon : Color;
        ButtonTextDisabled : Color;
        ButtonBackgroundHighlighted : Color;
        ButtonTextHighlighted : Color;
        ButtonBackgroundPressed : Color;
        ButtonTextPressed : Color;
    }

    var tuiColors : ColorPackage = {};

    func InstallColorPackage(colors : ColorPackage) : (void)
    {
        (tuiColors = colors);
    }

    func CreateBaselineDocument() : (DocumentModel^)
    {
        var document = new (DocumentModel^)();
        var normal = new (DocumentStyle^)();
        (normal.styles = new (DocumentStyleProperties^)());
        (normal.styles.color = tuiColors.ControlText);
        (normal.styles.backgroundColor = tuiColors.ControlBackground);
        (document.styles["#Default-Override"] = normal);
        var selection = new (DocumentStyle^)();
        (selection.styles = new (DocumentStyleProperties^)());
        (selection.styles.color = tuiColors.ItemTextSelected);
        (selection.styles.backgroundColor = tuiColors.ItemBackgroundSelected);
        (document.styles["#Selection-Override"] = selection);
        return document;
    }
}
